### [222] [GAIDE: Graph-based Attention Masking for Spatial- and Embodiment-aware Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04463)
*Davood Soleymanzadeh,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planning algorithms are widely used for motion planning of robotic manipulators, but they often struggle with sample inefficiency in high-dimensional configuration spaces due to their reliance on uniform or hand-crafted informed sampling primitives. Neural informed samplers address this limitation by learning the sampling distribution from prior planning experience to guide the motion planner towards planning goal. However, existing approaches often struggle to encode the spatial structure inherent in motion planning problems. To address this limitation, we introduce Graph-based Attention Masking for Spatial- and Embodiment-aware Motion Planning (GAIDE), a neural informed sampler that leverages both the spatial structure of the planning problem and the robotic manipulator's embodiment to guide the planning algorithm. GAIDE represents these structures as a graph and integrates it into a transformer-based neural sampler through attention masking. We evaluate GAIDE against baseline state-of-the-art sampling-based planners using uniform sampling, hand-crafted informed sampling, and neural informed sampling primitives. Evaluation results demonstrate that GAIDE improves planning efficiency and success rate.

</details>


### [223] [Act-Observe-Rewrite: Multimodal Coding Agents as In-Context Policy Learners for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04466)
*Vaishak Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AOR让LLM把“策略”当作可改写的低层控制代码：机器人每次失败后，模型看图和回合反馈重写Python控制器，再试一次。相比黑盒神经策略，这种可解释表示更利于定位系统性错误并修复；在3个robosuite任务上无需示范/奖励工程/训练更新也能达到高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖预定义技能库、一次性代码规划、示范数据或奖励设计，限制了策略可塑性。作者希望利用可解释代码作为策略载体，使模型不仅决定“做什么”，还能修改“怎么做”，以实现基于失败原因的内上下文学习。

Method: 提出AOR（Act-Observe-Rewrite）框架：每轮执行后基于视觉观测与结构化回合结果，让LLM直接合成新的可执行Python低层控制器代码，并在下一轮试验中部署，从而迭代改进策略。

Result: 在3个robosuite操作任务上，AOR在无示范、无奖励工程、无梯度更新条件下取得较高成功率，显示出通过失败诊断与代码重写实现有效策略改进的潜力。

Conclusion: 在特定可刻画条件下，多模态大语言模型可通过对失败的反思与代码重写，在无梯度更新、无示范、无奖励工程的前提下提升机器人操作策略。

Abstract: Can a multimodal language model learn to manipulate physical objects by reasoning about its own failures-without gradient updates, demonstrations, or reward engineering? We argue the answer is yes, under conditions we characterise precisely. We present Act-Observe-Rewrite (AOR), a framework in which an LLM agent improves a robot manipulation policy by synthesising entirely new executable Python controller code between trials, guided by visual observations and structured episode outcomes. Unlike prior work that grounds LLMs in pre-defined skill libraries or uses code generation for one-shot plan synthesis, AOR makes the full low-level motor control implementation the unit of LLM reasoning, enabling the agent to change not just what the robot does, but how it does it. The central claim is that interpretable code as the policy representation creates a qualitatively different kind of in-context learning from opaque neural policies: the agent can diagnose systematic failures and rewrite their causes. We validate this across three robosuite manipulation tasks and report promising results, with the agent achieving high success rates without demonstrations, reward engineering, or gradient updates.

</details>


### [224] [PTLD: Sim-to-real Privileged Tactile Latent Distillation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04531)
*Rosy Chen,Mustafa Mukadam,Michael Kaess,Tingfan Wu,Francois R Hogan,Jitendra Malik,Akash Sharma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 作者提出PTLD：不用做触觉仿真，而是用真实世界特权传感器数据蒸馏触觉状态估计器，增强仿真训练的本体感觉策略；在两项手内操作任务中分别取得182%与57%的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭复杂任务需要触觉灵巧操作，但高质量多指手演示数据难以通过遥操作或示教获得；纯强化学习虽可在仿真训练，但触觉观测难以快速且真实地仿真，导致sim-to-real受限。

Method: 提出PTLD（Privileged Tactile Latent Distillation）：先在仿真中训练本体感觉操作策略；再在真实世界利用特权传感器采集与触觉相关的数据；基于这些数据蒸馏出可由触觉输入驱动的稳健状态估计器，并将其融入控制策略，从而避免直接进行高保真触觉仿真。

Result: 在in-hand rotation基准任务上，相比仅本体感觉策略性能提升182%；在更具挑战性的触觉in-hand reorientation任务中，达到目标数量提升57%。

Conclusion: PTLD在无需触觉仿真的前提下，实现了触觉信息对灵巧操作策略的有效增益，并在仿真到现实迁移中显著优于仅本体感觉策略。

Abstract: Tactile dexterous manipulation is essential to automating complex household tasks, yet learning effective control policies remains a challenge. While recent work has relied on imitation learning, obtaining high quality demonstrations for multi-fingered hands via robot teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching is prohibitive. Alternatively, with reinforcement we can learn skills in simulation, but fast and realistic simulation of tactile observations is challenging. To bridge this gap, we introduce PTLD: sim-to-real Privileged Tactile Latent Distillation, a novel approach to learning tactile manipulation skills without requiring tactile simulation. Instead of simulating tactile sensors or relying purely on proprioceptive policies to transfer zero-shot sim-to-real, our key idea is to leverage privileged sensors in the real world to collect real-world tactile policy data. This data is then used to distill a robust state estimator that operates on tactile input. We demonstrate from our experiments that PTLD can be used to improve proprioceptive manipulation policies trained in simulation significantly by incorporating tactile sensing. On the benchmark in-hand rotation task, PTLD achieves a 182% improvement over a proprioception only policy. We also show that PTLD enables learning the challenging task of tactile in-hand reorientation where we see a 57% improvement in the number of goals reached over using proprioception alone. Website: .

</details>


### [225] [Many-RRT*: Robust Joint-Space Trajectory Planning for Serial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04547)
*Theodore M. Belmont,Benjamin A. Christie,Anton Netchaev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出Many-RRT*：对同一末端目标的多个IK解并行建树搜索，解决“选错关节空间目标”导致的失败与低质量路径问题；在不牺牲速度的前提下，轨迹质量和成功率大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自由度串联机械臂在关节空间规划时面临前向运动学不可逆导致的一对多映射问题：同一任务空间位姿对应多个关节构型。传统RRT若只选单一IK目标，容易因目标选择不佳导致路径质量差甚至规划失败，尤其在障碍复杂场景中更明显。

Method: 在RRT*-Connect基础上提出Many-RRT*：先为同一末端位姿生成多个IK解；以单一起始树配合多个目标树并行扩展；让不同目标构型同时参与搜索与连接，避免将计算预算集中在错误或次优关节空间目标上。

Result: 实验覆盖多种机器人构型与障碍环境。Many-RRT*在相同运行时间下轨迹代价降低44.5%，成功率达到100%，显著优于对比方法（次优仅1.6%），且未带来明显运行时开销。

Conclusion: Many-RRT*通过并行探索多个逆解目标，显著提升了高自由度机械臂在复杂环境中的规划成功率与轨迹质量，同时保持了与现有RRT系方法相当的运行时间，并保留收敛性与渐近最优性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of high degree-of-freedom (DoF) serial manipulators necessitates the use of swift, sampling-based motion planners for high-dimensional spaces. While sampling-based planners like the Rapidly-Exploring Random Tree (RRT) are widely used, planning in the manipulator's joint space presents significant challenges due to non-invertible forward kinematics. A single task-space end-effector pose can correspond to multiple configuration-space states, creating a multi-arm bandit problem for the planner. In complex environments, simply choosing the wrong joint space goal can result in suboptimal trajectories or even failure to find a viable plan. To address this planning problem, we propose Many-RRT*: an extension of RRT*-Connect that plans to multiple goals in parallel. By generating multiple IK solutions and growing independent trees from these goal configurations simultaneously alongside a single start tree, Many-RRT* ensures that computational effort is not wasted on suboptimal IK solutions. This approach maintains robust convergence and asymptotic optimality. Experimental evaluations across robot morphologies and diverse obstacle environments demonstrate that Many-RRT* provides higher quality trajectories (44.5% lower cost in the same runtime) with a significantly higher success rate (100% vs. the next best of 1.6%) than previous RRT iterations without compromising on runtime performance.

</details>


### [226] [From Local Corrections to Generalized Skills: Improving Neuro-Symbolic Policies with MEMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04560)
*Benjamin A. Christie,Yinlong Dai,Mohammad Bararjanianbahnamiri,Simon Stepputtis,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 MEMO：把人类纠错和成功经验做成可检索的“技能记忆库”，让机器人在执行时检索文本+代码来生成新技能。这样可突破固定技能库限制，并在未见任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号操作框架虽能将复杂任务分解为语义子任务，但受限于已有技能集合；当缺少匹配技能时策略会失败。作者希望利用人类在失败时给出的直观纠错，突破固定技能约束并实现技能的在线扩展。

Method: 提出 Memory Enhanced Manipulation（MEMO）：构建并维护一个由人类反馈与任务成功经验组成的检索增强 skillbook；运行时检索相关文本指导与代码片段，结合高层策略生成新技能；并对跨用户、跨任务的纠错语句进行收集、聚类与改写，形成更通用的指导和技能模板。

Result: 实验表明：MEMO 能将局部、一次性的人工反馈聚合为通用技能模板，相比基线在新任务泛化上表现更好，弥补了现有方法在技能覆盖不足时的性能缺口。

Conclusion: MEMO 通过汇聚多用户、多任务的自然语言纠错并转化为可检索的文本与代码技能模板，能够动态扩展机器人技能库，从而显著提升在新任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent works use a neuro-symbolic framework for general manipulation policies. The advantage of this framework is that -- by applying off-the-shelf vision and language models -- the robot can break complex tasks down into semantic subtasks. However, the fundamental bottleneck is that the robot needs skills to ground these subtasks into embodied motions. Skills can take many forms (e.g., trajectory snippets, motion primitives, coded functions), but regardless of their form skills act as a constraint. The high-level policy can only ground its language reasoning through the available skills; if the robot cannot generate the right skill for the current task, its policy will fail. We propose to address this limitation -- and dynamically expand the robot's skills -- by leveraging user feedback. When a robot fails, humans can intuitively explain what went wrong (e.g., ``no, go higher''). While a simple approach is to recall this exact text the next time the robot faces a similar situation, we hypothesize that by collecting, clustering, and re-phrasing natural language corrections across multiple users and tasks, we can synthesize more general text guidance and coded skill templates. Applying this hypothesis we develop Memory Enhanced Manipulation (MEMO). MEMO builds and maintains a retrieval-augmented skillbook gathered from human feedback and task successes. At run time, MEMO retrieves relevant text and code from this skillbook, enabling the robot's policy to generate new skills while reasoning over multi-task human feedback. Our experiments demonstrate that using MEMO to aggregate local feedback into general skill templates enables generalization to novel tasks where existing baselines fall short. See supplemental material here:

</details>


### [227] [Distributed State Estimation for Vision-Based Cooperative Slung Load Transportation in GPS-Denied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04571)
*Jack R. Pence,Jackson Fezell,Jack W. Langelaan,Junyi Geng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Transporting heavy or oversized slung loads using rotorcraft has traditionally relied on single-aircraft systems, which limits both payload capacity and control authority. Cooperative multilift using teams of rotorcraft offers a scalable and efficient alternative, especially for infrequent but challenging "long-tail" payloads without the need of building larger and larger rotorcraft. Most prior multilift research assumes GPS availability, uses centralized estimation architectures, or relies on controlled laboratory motion-capture setups. As a result, these methods lack robustness to sensor loss and are not viable in GPS-denied or operationally constrained environments. This paper addresses this limitation by presenting a distributed and decentralized payload state estimation framework for vision-based multilift operations. Using onboard monocular cameras, each UAV detects a fiducial marker on the payload and estimates its relative pose. These measurements are fused via a Distributed and Decentralized Extended Information Filter (DDEIF), enabling robust and scalable estimation that is resilient to individual sensor dropouts. This payload state estimate is then used for closed-loop trajectory tracking control. Monte Carlo simulation results in Gazebo show the effectiveness of the proposed approach, including the effect of communication loss during flight.

</details>


### [228] [Risk-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04579)
*Michael Groom,James Wilson,Nick Hawes,Lars Kunze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向移动操作的可运行时调节风险敏感度的深度视觉运动框架：先用分布式强化学习学到风险感知教师，再通过模仿学习蒸馏为仅依赖自中心深度输入的学生策略；结果在动态未知环境下显著提升最坏情况鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作机器人从实验室走向日常环境时，需要在动态、非结构化且存在不确定性的场景中进行风险感知决策；但现有全身控制器通常缺乏显式风险敏感机制。

Method: 方法分两阶段：先训练一个特权教师策略（使用Distributional RL与风险中性分布评论家），再对评论家预测的回报分布施加失真风险度量，计算风险调整优势以更新策略，从而得到不同风险偏好的教师；随后用模仿学习将教师行为蒸馏到仅依赖自中心深度观测的学生策略。

Result: 实验表明，所训练的视觉运动策略在未建图环境中可利用在线深度观测进行反应式全身运动，并呈现风险感知行为，尤其在最坏情况性能上更优。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在移动操作任务中，可通过分布式强化学习与模仿学习结合，学习到可调风险敏感的视觉运动策略，并在真实感知约束下实现更稳健的最坏情况表现。

Abstract: For robots to successfully transition from lab settings to everyday environments, they must begin to reason about the risks associated with their actions and make informed, risk-aware decisions. This is particularly true for robots performing mobile manipulation tasks, which involve both interacting with and navigating within dynamic, unstructured spaces. However, existing whole-body controllers for mobile manipulators typically lack explicit mechanisms for risk-sensitive decision-making under uncertainty. To our knowledge, we are the first to (i) learn risk-aware visuomotor policies for mobile manipulation conditioned on egocentric depth observations with runtime-adjustable risk sensitivity, and (ii) show risk-aware behaviours can be transferred through Imitation Learning (IL) to a visuomotor policy conditioned on egocentric depth observations. Our method achieves this by first training a privileged teacher policy using Distributional Reinforcement Learning (DRL), with a risk-neutral distributional critic. Distortion risk-metrics are then applied to the critic's predicted return distribution to calculate risk-adjusted advantage estimates used in policy updates to achieve a range of risk-aware behaviours. We then distil teacher policies with IL to obtain risk-aware student policies conditioned on egocentric depth observations. We perform extensive evaluations demonstrating that our trained visuomotor policies exhibit risk-aware behaviour (specifically achieving better worst-case performance) while performing reactive whole-body motions in unmapped environments, leveraging live depth observations for perception.

</details>


### [229] [ELLIPSE: Evidential Learning for Robust Waypoints and Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04585)
*Zihao Dong,Chanyoung Chung,Dong-Ki Kim,Mukhtar Maulimov,Xiangyun Meng,Harmish Khambhaita,Ali-akbar Agha-mohammadi,Amirreza Shaban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELLIPSE=“会给答案也会给靠谱置信度”的IL航点预测：用证据回归+Student-t一次性输出航点与不确定性；用轻量扰动增强抵抗协变量偏移；再用PIT同位回归做后校准。真实机器人楼梯实验中，成功率和不确定性覆盖都优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IL在已见分布内表现好，但遇到陌生状态会因分布偏移而过度自信，安全关键场景（如楼梯）风险高。作者希望在不额外采集示范数据的前提下，同时提升航点精度、成功率与不确定性可靠性。

Method: 方法包含三部分：1) 基于多变量深度证据回归（multivariate deep evidential regression）学习航点及其不确定性，预测分布采用多变量 Student-t；2) 设计轻量域增强，在专家轨迹附近合成视角/位姿扰动样本，以缓解协变量偏移导致的过置信；3) 在部署前对PIT值进行后处理同位回归（isotonic recalibration），提高环境/域外偏移下的不确定性校准。实验任务聚焦楼梯场景航点预测。

Result: 大量真实世界评估表明，与基线相比，ELLIPSE 在任务成功率和不确定性覆盖率上均有提升，且在视角/位姿扰动及未见环境（如未见楼梯）下表现更稳健。

Conclusion: ELLIPSE 通过单次前向推理同时输出航点与多变量 Student-t 预测分布，并结合轻量域增强与PIT同位回归重校准，可在开放世界、分布偏移场景下提升移动机器人航点预测的鲁棒性与不确定性可信度。

Abstract: Robust waypoint prediction is crucial for mobile robots operating in open-world, safety-critical settings. While Imitation Learning (IL) methods have demonstrated great success in practice, they are susceptible to distribution shifts: the policy can become dangerously overconfident in unfamiliar states. In this paper, we present \textit{ELLIPSE}, a method building on multivariate deep evidential regression to output waypoints and multivariate Student-t predictive distributions in a single forward pass. To reduce covariate-shift-induced overconfidence under viewpoint and pose perturbations near expert trajectories, we introduce a lightweight domain augmentation procedure that synthesizes plausible viewpoint/pose variations without collecting additional demonstrations. To improve uncertainty reliability under environment/domain shift (e.g., unseen staircases), we apply a post-hoc isotonic recalibration on probability integral transform (PIT) values so that prediction sets remain plausible during deployment. We ground the discussion and experiments in staircase waypoint prediction, where obtaining robust waypoint and uncertainty is pivotal. Extensive real world evaluations show that \textit{ELLIPSE} improves both task success rate and uncertainty coverage compared to baselines.

</details>


### [230] [RoboMME: Benchmarking and Understanding Memory for Robotic Generalist Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04639)
*Yinpei Dai,Hongze Fu,Jayjun Lee,Yuejiang Liu,Haoran Zhang,Jianing Yang,Chelsea Finn,Nima Fazeli,Joyce Chai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出RoboMME：一个面向长时程、历史依赖机器人操作的标准化记忆基准（16任务、四类记忆维度），并在π0.5上构建14种记忆增强VLA模型进行系统评测。结论是：记忆设计强任务依赖，不存在统一最优方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA虽开始引入记忆，但评测场景狭窄且缺乏标准化，导致难以系统理解模型能力、横向公平比较及持续衡量进展；而长时程操作（如计数重复动作、遮挡下操控）又高度依赖记忆。

Method: 提出RoboMME基准：包含16个操作任务，按时间、空间、对象、流程四类记忆能力进行分类评测；并基于π0.5骨干实现14种带记忆增强的VLA变体，系统比较多种记忆表示与集成方式。

Result: 实验表明记忆表示的效果显著依赖任务类型；各方案在不同任务上各有优劣，体现出明显的能力-场景匹配关系。

Conclusion: RoboMME为长期、历史依赖的机器人操作提供了首个较大规模、标准化的记忆评测框架；记忆机制没有“一招通吃”的方案，不同任务需要匹配不同记忆表示与集成策略。

Abstract: Memory is critical for long-horizon and history-dependent robotic manipulation. Such tasks often involve counting repeated actions or manipulating objects that become temporarily occluded. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models have begun to incorporate memory mechanisms; however, their evaluations remain confined to narrow, non-standardized settings. This limits their systematic understanding, comparison, and progress measurement. To address these challenges, we introduce RoboMME: a large-scale standardized benchmark for evaluating and advancing VLA models in long-horizon, history-dependent scenarios. Our benchmark comprises 16 manipulation tasks constructed under a carefully designed taxonomy that evaluates temporal, spatial, object, and procedural memory. We further develop a suite of 14 memory-augmented VLA variants built on the {\pi}0.5 backbone to systematically explore different memory representations across multiple integration strategies. Experimental results show that the effectiveness of memory representations is highly task-dependent, with each design offering distinct advantages and limitations across different tasks. Videos and code can be found at our website .

</details>


### [231] [Autonomous Aerial Non-Destructive Testing: Ultrasound Inspection with a Commercial Quadrotor in an Unstructured Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04642)
*Ruben Veenstra,Barbara Bazzana,Sander Smits,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 作者把原本只能遥控的商用无人机改造成可自主“贴靠接触”检测的平台：通过多速率控制与机载传感闭环，在Elios 3上实现了仅靠机载传感的自主超声NDT，证明了商用平台可被低成本改造用于危险/狭窄场景的接触式巡检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统商用多旋翼通常仅支持遥控，难以在危险、封闭、非结构化工业环境中稳定执行接触式无损检测；因此需要一种可在现成平台上实现自主物理交互的系统架构。

Method: 提出一套集成控制与软件架构：直接对接现成无人机机载传感器，采用多速率控制（200 Hz底层控制、100 Hz力估计、50 Hz导纳滤波与轨迹规划），并向内部飞控发送加速度与偏航角速度指令；在搭载超声载荷的Elios 3上进行实物实验验证。

Result: 在仅依赖机载感知的条件下，四旋翼在类工业非结构化环境中成功完成自主超声NDT接触测量，验证了该控制与软件框架在真实物理接触任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过改造商用多旋翼平台并接入机载传感器，可实现安全、可行的全自主接触式NDT，为危险和狭窄基础设施的自动化近接触检测提供了现实路径。

Abstract: This work presents an integrated control and software architecture that enables arguably the first fully autonomous, contact-based non-destructive testing (NDT) using a commercial multirotor originally restricted to remotely-piloted operations. To allow autonomous operation with an off-the-shelf platform, we developed a real-time framework that interfaces directly with its onboard sensor suite. The architecture features a multi-rate control scheme: low-level control is executed at 200 Hz, force estimation at 100 Hz, while an admittance filter and trajectory planner operate at 50 Hz, ultimately supplying acceleration and yaw rate commands to the internal flight controller. We validate the system through physical experiments on a Flyability Elios 3 quadrotor equipped with an ultrasound payload. Relying exclusively on onboard sensing, the vehicle successfully performs autonomous NDT measurements within an unstructured, industrial-like environment. This work demonstrates the viability of retrofitting off-the-shelf platforms for autonomous physical interaction, paving the way for safe, contact-based inspection of hazardous and confined infrastructure.

</details>


### [232] [Python Bindings for a Large C++ Robotics Library: The Case of OMPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04668)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文验证了LLM可辅助生成nanobind：按代码结构搭脚手架→LLM生成→专家把关。虽会在shared_ptr、重载和trampoline上出错，但通过上下文示例与精心提示可提升可靠性，最终性能不输传统手写/既有绑定方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型高性能C++库需要Python绑定以支持机器人研究中的快速原型、复现实验与生态集成，但手工为大规模代码库编写/维护绑定成本高、负担集中在少数维护者身上。

Method: 提出面向大型C++代码库的绑定生成工作流：先按代码结构脚手架化空的nanobind包装文件，再由LLM补全绑定定义，最后由专家审阅与修正；并通过案例分析总结失败模式，结合in-context示例与提示词优化提升生成可靠性。

Result: 在大型C++运动规划库案例中，识别了共享指针、函数重载、trampoline等常见错误；通过示例驱动与提示工程可显著改善生成质量；最终绑定运行时性能与传统方案相当。

Conclusion: 在大型C++项目中，采用“LLM生成+专家审核”的人机协同流程可以有效降低Python绑定开发负担，并在保证正确性与性能的前提下达到可用的工程效果。

Abstract: Python bindings are a critical bridge between high-performance C++ libraries and the flexibility of Python, enabling rapid prototyping, reproducible experiments, and integration with simulation and learning frameworks in robotics research. Yet, generating bindings for large codebases is a tedious process that creates a heavy burden for a small group of maintainers. In this work, we investigate the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist in generating nanobind wrappers, with human experts kept in the loop. Our workflow mirrors the structure of the C++ codebase, scaffolds empty wrapper files, and employs LLMs to fill in binding definitions. Experts then review and refine the generated code to ensure correctness, compatibility, and performance. Through a case study on a large C++ motion planning library, we document common failure modes, including mismanaging shared pointers, overloads, and trampolines, and show how in-context examples and careful prompt design improve reliability. Experiments demonstrate that the resulting bindings achieve runtime performance comparable to legacy solutions. Beyond this case study, our results provide general lessons for applying LLMs to binding generation in large-scale C++ projects.

</details>


### [233] [Selecting Spots by Explicitly Predicting Intention from Motion History Improves Performance in Autonomous Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04695)
*Long Kiu Chung,David Isele,Faizan M. Tariq,Sangjae Bae,Shreyas Kousik,Jovin D'sa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于自动代客泊车的“显式意图预测+概率信念图”框架：利用他车历史轨迹预测其将停向何处，再进行车位选择与协商。含遮挡和预测误差的仿真结果显示，该方法较现有隐式/短期预测方法更准确、更符合社会规范、任务完成更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会导航研究表明意图推理有助于安全与可接受决策，但在AVP中，车辆需在规则更宽松、交互更复杂的停车场完成从下客到找位、协商、泊车的全流程，单纯短时运动预测难以可靠推断他车长期停车目标。

Method: 提出一条AVP决策管线：先基于其他车辆的运动历史，通过学习模型与概率信念图显式预测其可能停车意图，再据此选择车位并进行博弈式协商与泊车；并构建含反应式智能体的仿真环境，加入遮挡感知观测与不完美轨迹预测等现实假设进行评测。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法在意图/目标预测精度、社会接受度及任务完成率方面优于两类基线：①从未来预测轨迹反推意图的方法；②端到端隐式嵌入意图的方法。

Conclusion: 在自动代客泊车（AVP）场景中，相比仅依赖短时轨迹预测或端到端隐式建模，基于历史运动进行显式意图预测更能处理停车场中长期、多样且歧义性强的目标推理，从而提升安全性、社会接受度与任务完成效果。

Abstract: In many applications of social navigation, existing works have shown that predicting and reasoning about human intentions can help robotic agents make safer and more socially acceptable decisions. In this work, we study this problem for autonomous valet parking (AVP), where an autonomous vehicle ego agent must drop off its passengers, explore the parking lot, find a parking spot, negotiate for the spot with other vehicles, and park in the spot without human supervision. Specifically, we propose an AVP pipeline that selects parking spots by explicitly predicting where other agents are going to park from their motion history using learned models and probabilistic belief maps. To test this pipeline, we build a simulation environment with reactive agents and realistic modeling assumptions on the ego agent, such as occlusion-aware observations, and imperfect trajectory prediction. Simulation experiments show that our proposed method outperforms existing works that infer intentions from future predicted motion or embed them implicitly in end-to-end models, yielding better results in prediction accuracy, social acceptance, and task completion. Our key insight is that, in parking, where driving regulations are more lax, explicit intention prediction is crucial for reasoning about diverse and ambiguous long-term goals, which cannot be reliably inferred from short-term motion prediction alone, but can be effectively learned from motion history.

</details>


### [234] [LEGS-POMDP: Language and Gesture-Guided Object Search in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04705)
*Ivy Xiao He,Stefanie Tellex,Jason Xinyu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出LEGS-POMDP：把语言+手势+视觉接入POMDP，并同时跟踪“找什么”和“在哪儿”两种不确定性。结果是开放场景找物体更稳，仿真平均成功率89%，实机验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环境下人类指令常含歧义，机器人需要在长时序任务中处理多源不确定性。基础模型虽擅长多模态对齐，但缺乏可解释、可规划的不确定性机制；传统POMDP虽擅长不确定规划，却常受模态支持不足和环境假设过强限制。

Method: 提出模块化LEGS-POMDP框架：以POMDP进行长时域不确定规划，状态中显式表示(1)目标物体身份不确定性与(2)目标空间位置不确定性；观测层融合语言指令、手势线索和视觉感知，多模态更新信念并驱动搜索与操作决策。

Result: 仿真中，多模态融合相较单模态基线显著更优，在具有挑战性的环境与类别上平均成功率达89%；在四足移动操作机器人上的真实实验定性展示了在歧义指令下的稳健多模态感知与不确定性收敛能力。

Conclusion: LEGS-POMDP通过在POMDP中联合建模目标身份与空间位置两类不确定性，并融合语言、手势与视觉信息，可在开放世界目标搜索中显著提升鲁棒性与成功率；仿真与真实机器人实验均验证了其实用性。

Abstract: To assist humans in open-world environments, robots must interpret ambiguous instructions to locate desired objects. Foundation model-based approaches excel at multimodal grounding, but they lack a principled mechanism for modeling uncertainty in long-horizon tasks. In contrast, Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) provide a systematic framework for planning under uncertainty but are often limited in supported modalities and rely on restrictive environment assumptions. We introduce LanguagE and Gesture-Guided Object Search in Partially Observable Environments (LEGS-POMDP), a modular POMDP system that integrates language, gesture, and visual observations for open-world object search. Unlike prior work, LEGS-POMDP explicitly models two sources of partial observability: uncertainty over the target object's identity and its spatial location. In simulation, multimodal fusion significantly outperforms unimodal baselines, achieving an average success rate of 89\% across challenging environments and object categories. Finally, we demonstrate the full system on a quadruped mobile manipulator, where real-world experiments qualitatively validate robust multimodal perception and uncertainty reduction under ambiguous instructions.

</details>


### [235] [Design, Mapping, and Contact Anticipation with 3D-printed Whole-Body Tactile and Proximity Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04714)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Anna Soukhovei,Caleb Escobedo,Nataliya Nechyporenko,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Robots operating in dynamic and shared environments benefit from anticipating contact before it occurs. We present GenTact-Prox, a fully 3D-printed artificial skin that integrates tactile and proximity sensing for contact detection and anticipation. The artificial skin platform is modular in design, procedurally generated to fit any robot morphology, and can cover the whole body of a robot. The skin achieved detection ranges of up to 18 cm during evaluation. To characterize how robots perceive nearby space through this skin, we introduce a data-driven framework for mapping the Perisensory Space -- the body-centric volume of space around the robot where sensors provide actionable information for contact anticipation. We demonstrate this approach on a Franka Research 3 robot equipped with five GenTact-Prox units, enabling online object-aware operation and contact prediction.

</details>


### [236] [Gait Generation Balancing Joint Load and Mobility for Legged Modular Robots with Easily Detachable Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04757)
*Kennosuke Chihara,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇工作用NSGA-III做多目标步态优化，不再只追求“走得快/稳”，还同时压低关节扭矩；结果是在斜坡和台阶等场景中依然保持可用机动性，并减少关节过载导致的失效风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化机器人虽然适应性强，但在运动过程中关节扭矩过大容易导致机械失效，尤其是可拆卸关节；传统方法偏重机动性，忽视关节载荷风险，因此需要一种兼顾运动性能与结构安全的优化策略。

Method: 提出一种以NSGA-III为核心的多目标步态优化方法，将“最小化关节负载”与“满足运动速度和稳定性约束”联合建模，求取Pareto最优解集，并在仿真与实体实验中验证其在斜坡、台阶等环境下的步态生成能力。

Result: 仿真和实体验证表明，该方法可在多种复杂地形下生成有效步态，并在不明显牺牲整体机动性的前提下降低关节负载，提升结构完整性与运行可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于NSGA-III的多目标优化框架能够在保证机动性（速度与稳定性）的同时显著降低模块化机器人关节负载，从而提升可拆卸关节与整体结构的可靠性。

Abstract: While modular robots offer versatility, excessive joint torque during locomotion poses a significant risk of mechanical failure, especially for detachable joints. To address this, we propose an optimization framework using the NSGA-III algorithm. Unlike conventional approaches that prioritize mobility alone, our method derives Pareto optimal solutions to minimize joint load while maintaining necessary locomotion speed and stability. Simulations and physical experiments demonstrate that our approach successfully generates gait motions for diverse environments, such as slopes and steps, ensuring structural integrity without compromising overall mobility.

</details>


### [237] [Designing and Validating a Self-Aligning Tool Changer for Modular Reconfigurable Manipulation Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04760)
*Mahfudz Maskur,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇工作提出一种“抗对位误差”的机器人自动换工具系统：用倒角与三角导向实现被动自对准，配合电机驱动耦合和旋转式工具库，在不依赖复杂力传感的情况下，显著提高了自主换装成功率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化可重构机器人在自动更换模块时常因不可避免的位置与姿态误差导致传统刚性主动耦合失败，因此需要一种无需复杂传感、对失配更鲁棒的自动换工具机制。

Method: 设计并实现一种电机驱动的换工具耦合机构，在机械端引入倒角插座与三角导向等被动自对准几何以补偿角度和横向偏差；并配套紧凑型旋转式工具交换站用于模块存储与取放，随后通过真实自主取工具实验进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，自对准特征能够有效吸收执行误差，在真实场景下实现高成功率的自主取工具与重构过程，显著提升换装可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的容错型换工具系统通过被动自对准结构与电机驱动耦合设计，可在存在位姿误差时实现高可靠的自动模块更换，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Modular reconfigurable robots require reliable mechanisms for automated module exchange, but conventional rigid active couplings often fail due to inevitable positioning and orientational errors. To address this, we propose a misalignment-tolerant tool-changing system. The hardware features a motor-driven coupling utilizing passive self-alignment geometries, specifically chamfered receptacles and triangular lead-in guides, to robustly compensate for angular and lateral misalignments without complex force sensors. To make this autonomous exchange practically feasible, the mechanism is complemented by a compact rotating tool exchange station for efficient module storage. Real-world autonomous tool-picking experiments validate that the self-aligning features successfully absorb execution errors, enabling highly reliable robotic tool reconfiguration.

</details>


### [238] [Adaptive Policy Switching of Two-Wheeled Differential Robots for Traversing over Diverse Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04761)
*Haruki Izawa,Takeshi Takai,Shingo Kitano,Mikita Miyaguchi,Hiroaki Kawashima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 作者证明：仅用机器人短时间的姿态数据（特别是俯仰角波动）就能高精度识别地形。方法是PPO微调策略后采集姿态、用GMM分类；在70步窗口上准确率>98%，可支撑后续自适应策略切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练控制策略难以覆盖熔岩管道中所有未知地形，机器人需要在无人工干预下根据当前地形自动切换到合适的专用策略，因此需要一种可靠、轻量的地形识别信号。

Method: 在模拟熔岩管环境中，先用PPO对预训练策略进行微调；让机器人在平坦与粗糙地形上运行并采集3D姿态数据；提取俯仰角统计特征（重点是标准差），再用高斯混合模型在不同时间窗长度下进行地形分类评估。

Result: 俯仰角标准差在平坦与粗糙地形之间存在明显可分性；使用GMM进行分类时，70步时间窗可达到98%以上准确率。

Conclusion: 基于导航过程中短时姿态观测（尤其是俯仰角波动）可以高精度估计地形类型，为月球熔岩管道中的自适应策略切换提供可行基础。

Abstract: Exploring lunar lava tubes requires robots to traverse without human intervention. Because pre-trained policies cannot fully cover all possible terrain conditions, our goal is to enable adaptive policy switching, where the robot selects an appropriate terrain-specialized model based on its current terrain features. This study investigates whether terrain types can be estimated effectively using posture-related observations collected during navigation. We fine-tuned a pre-trained policy using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), and then collected the robot's 3D orientation data as it moved across flat and rough terrain in a simulated lava-tube environment. Our analysis revealed that the standard deviation of the robot's pitch data shows a clear difference between these two terrain types. Using Gaussian mixture models (GMM), we evaluated terrain classification across various window sizes. An accuracy of more than 98% was achieved when using a 70-step window. The result suggests that short-term orientation data are sufficient for reliable terrain estimation, providing a foundation for adaptive policy switching.

</details>

*Hiroaki Kawashima,Shun Ikejima,Takeshi Takai,Mikita Miyaguchi,Yasuharu Kunii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Abstract: When individual robots have limited sensing capabilities or insufficient fault tolerance, it becomes necessary for multiple robots to form teams during exploration, thereby increasing the collective observation range and reliability. Traditionally, swarm formation has often been managed by a central controller; however, from the perspectives of robustness and flexibility, it is preferable for the swarm to operate autonomously even in the absence of centralized control. In addition, the determination of exploration targets for each team is crucial for efficient exploration in such multi-team exploration scenarios. This study therefore proposes an exploration method that combines (1) an algorithm for self-organization, enabling the autonomous and dynamic formation of multiple teams, and (2) an algorithm that allows each team to autonomously determine its next exploration target (destination). In particular, for (2), this study explores a novel strategy based on large language models (LLMs), while classical frontier-based methods and deep reinforcement learning approaches have been widely studied. The effectiveness of the proposed method was validated through simulations involving tens to hundreds of robots.

</details>


### [240] [On the Strengths and Weaknesses of Data for Open-set Embodied Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04819)
*Pradyumna Tambwekar,Andrew Silva,Deepak Gopinath,Jonathan DeCastro,Xiongyi Cui,Guy Rosman*
